Snap & Share V3 — design spec (all boards)

For Claude Code: every board below is the full source of one artboard from the design canvas. Styles are inline; per-theme colours come from the tokens CSS (classes .light / .dark, which map to :root / .dark in the app). <sc-for> = loop, <sc-if> = conditional, {{x}} = values from the renderVals() script at the bottom of each board (that's the sample data). Photo tiles (.p-or etc.) are placeholders for real images. Flow diagrams describe every path, including errors (red dashed = negative path).

Tokens (theme CSS)

/* Snap & Share v2 — "Gallery" direction. Neutral UI, vivid photos. :root = .light, .dark = dark */
.light{
  --bg:#F4F4F2; --bg-2:#ECECE9; --surface:#FFFFFF; --surface-2:#F0F0ED; --surface-3:#E6E6E2;
  --line:#E2E2DE; --line-strong:#CFCFCA;
  --ink:#0E0E10; --ink-2:#55555B; --ink-3:#6B6B71; --mute:#8A8A90;
  --btn:#0E0E10; --btn-ink:#FFFFFF;
  --accent:#FF5A1F; --accent-ink:#0E0E10; --blue:#2B2BFF;
  --live:#0F7A3D; --warn-bg:#FFF1E8; --warn-line:#FFC9AE; --warn-ink:#7A2A05;
  --danger:#C01F2F; --danger-bg:#FDECEE;
  --ring:0 0 0 2px #F4F4F2,0 0 0 4px #0E0E10;
  --sh-1:0 1px 2px rgba(14,14,16,.05),0 2px 8px rgba(14,14,16,.04);
  --sh-2:0 30px 60px -24px rgba(14,14,16,.22),0 8px 20px -8px rgba(14,14,16,.10);
  --glass:rgba(244,244,242,.78);
  color-scheme:light;
}
.dark{
  --bg:#0C0C0D; --bg-2:#111113; --surface:#161618; --surface-2:#1E1E21; --surface-3:#26262A;
  --line:#26262A; --line-strong:#3A3A3F;
  --ink:#F4F4F2; --ink-2:#B6B6BC; --ink-3:#8F8F96; --mute:#6E6E75;
  --btn:#F4F4F2; --btn-ink:#0E0E10;
  --accent:#FF6A33; --accent-ink:#0E0E10; --blue:#5B5BFF;
  --live:#4ADE80; --warn-bg:rgba(255,106,51,.10); --warn-line:rgba(255,106,51,.35); --warn-ink:#FFB898;
  --danger:#FF8A94; --danger-bg:rgba(255,90,100,.12);
  --ring:0 0 0 2px #0C0C0D,0 0 0 4px #F4F4F2;
  --sh-1:0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,.4),0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.3);
  --sh-2:0 30px 60px -24px rgba(0,0,0,.7),0 8px 20px -8px rgba(0,0,0,.5);
  --glass:rgba(12,12,13,.78);
  color-scheme:dark;
}
.light,.dark{background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);font-family:'Geist',system-ui,sans-serif;-webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased}
.hd{font-family:'Geist',system-ui,sans-serif;letter-spacing:-0.045em;font-weight:500}

.btn:hover{opacity:.88}.btn:active,.ib:active{transform:scale(.96)}
.ghost:hover,.ib:hover{background:var(--surface-3)!important}
.chip:active{transform:scale(.94)}
.btn:focus-visible,.chip:focus-visible,.tile:focus-visible,.ib:focus-visible,.fld:focus-visible,.lnk:focus-visible{outline:none;box-shadow:var(--ring)}

.fld:focus{border-color:var(--ink)!important}
.tile:hover{transform:translateY(-4px) rotate(-.4deg)}.tile:active{transform:scale(.98)}
.deck>*{transition:transform .35s cubic-bezier(.2,.9,.3,1.2)}
.arrive{animation:arrive .7s cubic-bezier(.2,.9,.3,1.2) both}
@keyframes arrive{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(16px) rotate(4deg) scale(.92)}to{opacity:1}}
.pulse{animation:pulse 1.8s ease-out infinite}
@keyframes pulse{0%{box-shadow:0 0 0 0 rgba(34,197,94,.55)}70%{box-shadow:0 0 0 7px rgba(34,197,94,0)}100%{box-shadow:0 0 0 0 rgba(34,197,94,0)}}
.bar{background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--ink) 0%,var(--ink-2) 50%,var(--ink) 100%);background-size:200% 100%;animation:flow 1.4s linear infinite}
@keyframes flow{from{background-position:0 0}to{background-position:200% 0}}
.bob{animation:bob 5s ease-in-out infinite}
@keyframes bob{0%,100%{translate:0 0}50%{translate:0 -5px}}

<script type="text/x-dc" data-dc-script data-props='{"$preview":{"width":1330,"height":1040}}'>
class Component extends DCLogic {
  renderVals() {
    const q = ['10', '25', '50', 'No limit'];
    return {
      quick: q.map((l) => ({ label: l, checked: l === '25' ? 'true' : 'false', bg: l === '25' ? 'var(--btn)' : 'var(--surface)', ink: l === '25' ? 'var(--btn-ink)' : 'var(--ink)', border: l === '25' ? '0' : '1px solid var(--line-strong)' }))

class Component extends DCLogic {
  renderVals() {
    const k = [
      { l: '30 days', h: 'Shortest, least storage' }, { l: '90 days', h: 'Default — enough time to download', on: true },

    ];

      keep: k.map((x, i) => ({ label: x.l, help: x.h, checked: x.on ? 'true' : 'false', border: i === 0 ? '0' : '1px solid var(--line)', dot: x.on ? '7px solid var(--ink)' : '2px solid var(--line-strong)' }))
    };

<ul style="margin: 12px 0 0; padding: 0 0 0 18px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 9px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.4; color: #D6D6DA">
<li><strong style="color: #fff; font-weight: 600">Strip GPS/EXIF</strong> on upload — photos can leak a home address.</li>
<li><strong style="color: #fff; font-weight: 600">Private storage + signed URLs</strong> so a photo link can't be shared outside the album.</li>
<li><strong style="color: #fff; font-weight: 600">Guests delete their own</strong> photos and comments (needed for privacy law).</li>
<li><strong style="color: #fff; font-weight: 600">Report button</strong> on every photo/comment → review queue.</li>
<li><strong style="color: #fff; font-weight: 600">Rate limits</strong> per pass: e.g. 50 uploads/hour, 1 comment / 5 s.</li>
<li><strong style="color: #fff; font-weight: 600">Remove + block</strong> a guest revokes their pass instantly.</li>
<li><strong style="color: #fff; font-weight: 600">Album expiry</strong> — auto-delete after 90 days unless the host keeps it.</li>
<li><strong style="color: #fff; font-weight: 600">One-line consent</strong> on join: "Photos you add are visible to this event's guests."</li>
<li><strong style="color: #fff; font-weight: 600">Hash pass words</strong> like passwords; 5 failed tries = 1 min cooldown.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</x-dc>
<script type="text/x-dc" data-dc-script data-props='{"$preview":{"width":1440,"height":1040}}'>

      ],

      })

class Component extends DCLogic {
  renderVals() {
    const names = [['bg','--bg'],['surface','--surface'],['surface-2','--surface-2'],['line','--line-strong'],['ink','--ink'],['ink-2','--ink-2'],['mute','--mute'],['btn','--btn'],['accent','--accent'],['blue','--blue']];
    const L = ['#F4F4F2','#FFFFFF','#F0F0ED','#CFCFCA','#0E0E10','#55555B','#8A8A90','#0E0E10','#FF5A1F','#2B2BFF'];
    const D = ['#0C0C0D','#161618','#1E1E21','#3A3A3F','#F4F4F2','#B6B6BC','#6E6E75','#F4F4F2','#FF6A33','#5B5BFF'];
    const mk = (hexes) => names.map((n, i) => ({ n: n[0], v: 'var(' + n[1] + ')', hex: hexes[i] }));
    return {
      themes: [
        { cls: 'light', label: 'Light', sw: mk(L) },
        { cls: 'dark', label: 'Dark', sw: mk(D) }
      ]
    };
  }
}

<span class="mono" style="font-size: 11px; color: var(--ink-3)">Tokens + Tailwind mapping: see V2 tokens board</span>

<section style="padding: 22px; border-radius: 24px; background: var(--surface); border: 1px solid var(--line)">
<div class="mono" style="font-size: 11px; color: var(--ink-3)">01 · Screens &amp; routes</div>
<ol style="margin: 14px 0 0; padding: 0 0 0 18px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 11px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.45; color: var(--ink-2)">
<li><strong style="color: var(--ink); font-weight: 600">/e/[id]</strong> — guest album. Tabs: Album (3-col 4:5 grid, prompt circles filter) · Feed. Bottom bar: Album · + · Feed.</li>
<li><strong style="color: var(--ink); font-weight: 600">Feed</strong> — guests row (ring = uploaded in last 15 min), posts with like, comment, share, download.</li>
<li><strong style="color: var(--ink); font-weight: 600">?photo=[pid]</strong> — story viewer. Tap right/left = next/prev, swipe down = close, like + comment bar.</li>
<li><strong style="color: var(--ink); font-weight: 600">Comments sheet</strong> — bottom sheet, 6 quick emoji, name remembered from upload.</li>
<li><strong style="color: var(--ink); font-weight: 600">Upload composer</strong> — multi-select, one prompt + name for the batch, posts in sequence with progress in feed.</li>
<li><strong style="color: var(--ink); font-weight: 600">/host/[id]?t=</strong> — same profile layout + likes stat, counts on tiles, QR card, remove photos &amp; comments.</li>
</ol>
</section>

<section style="padding: 22px; border-radius: 24px; background: #161618; color: #F4F4F2; display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-height: 0">
<div class="mono" style="font-size: 11px; color: #8F8F96">02 · Supabase · new migration 0005_likes_comments.sql</div>
<pre class="mono" style="margin: 14px 0 0; flex: 1; font-size: 11.5px; line-height: 1.55; text-transform: none; letter-spacing: 0; white-space: pre-wrap; color: #E6E6E2; overflow: hidden">create table photo_likes (
  photo_id   uuid references photos(id) on delete cascade,
  device_id  text not null,   -- random id in localStorage
  created_at timestamptz default now(),
  primary key (photo_id, device_id)
);

create table photo_comments (
  id         uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  photo_id   uuid references photos(id) on delete cascade,
  guest_name text check (char_length(guest_name) &lt;= 40),
  body       text not null check (char_length(body) between 1 and 300),
  created_at timestamptz default now()
);

-- RLS: anon select + insert only when the photo's event
--   is_active (same check as photos).
-- Unlike: rpc unlike_photo(photo_id, device_id).
-- Remove comment: rpc host_delete_comment(comment_id, host_token),
--   mirroring 0004_add_photo_delete.

alter publication supabase_realtime
  add table photo_likes, photo_comments;

create view photo_stats as
  select p.id, count(distinct l.device_id) likes,
         count(distinct c.id) comments
  from photos p
  left join photo_likes l on l.photo_id = p.id
  left join photo_comments c on c.photo_id = p.id
  group by p.id;</pre>
</section>

<section style="padding: 22px; border-radius: 24px; background: var(--surface); border: 1px solid var(--line); display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 14px">
<div class="mono" style="font-size: 11px; color: var(--ink-3)">03 · Behaviour rules</div>
<ul style="margin: 0; padding: 0 0 0 18px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 10px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.45; color: var(--ink-2)">
<li><strong style="color: var(--ink); font-weight: 600">Likes are optimistic.</strong> Heart fills orange and the count ticks up at once; roll back with a quiet toast if the insert fails.</li>
<li><strong style="color: var(--ink); font-weight: 600">Double-tap</strong> a photo to like (never to unlike). White heart burst, 900 ms; off for reduced motion.</li>
<li><strong style="color: var(--ink); font-weight: 600">Comments</strong> appear live with the arrive animation. Max 300 chars, plain text only, links not clickable.</li>
<li><strong style="color: var(--ink); font-weight: 600">Identity</strong> is the guest name from upload + device id. Anyone can fake it — fine for a party; host moderation is the safety valve.</li>
<li><strong style="color: var(--ink); font-weight: 600">Counts</strong> come from photo_stats; realtime events patch them in place.</li>
<li><strong style="color: var(--ink); font-weight: 600">A11y</strong> — heart is a real button with aria-pressed and a label including the count; sheet traps focus; 44 px targets everywhere.</li>
</ul>
</section>

</div>
</div>

class Component extends DCLogic {
  renderVals() {
    const now = { tag: 'MVP', tagBg: 'var(--btn)', tagInk: 'var(--btn-ink)' };
    const next = { tag: 'Next', tagBg: 'var(--surface-3)', tagInk: 'var(--ink-2)' };
    const mk = (base, t, d) => Object.assign({}, base, { t: t, d: d });
    return {
      cols: [
        { title: '01 · Privacy & storage', bg: 'var(--surface)', ink: 'var(--ink)', sub: 'var(--ink-3)', items: [
          mk(now, 'Make the event-photos bucket private', 'Today 0001_init.sql creates it public. Flip to private and serve via createSignedUrl (e.g. 1 h), refreshed by the client.'),
          mk(now, 'Strip GPS / EXIF before upload', 'browser-image-compression re-encodes on a canvas, which normally drops EXIF — keep preserveExif off and add a test so it stays that way.'),
          mk(now, 'Guests delete their own content', 'photos.pass_id + comments.pass_id; RLS allows delete where pass_id = current pass.'),
          mk(now, 'Consent record', 'consents(pass_id, event_id, version, accepted_at). Shown once per pass.'),
          mk(now, 'Retention job', 'events.delete_after (default created_at + 90 d). Daily cron: email host at −14 / −3 d, then delete rows + storage.')
        ] },
        { title: '02 · Access & abuse', bg: 'var(--surface)', ink: 'var(--ink)', sub: 'var(--ink-3)', items: [
          mk(now, 'Code mode is the default', 'events.access_mode: open | code | approval, default code. 6-char code, no 0/O/1/I.'),
          mk(now, 'Host sign-in with recovery', 'Supabase Auth: email magic link + pass words as password. Hosts never depend on pass words alone.'),
          mk(now, 'Rate limits', '50 uploads / h and 1 comment / 5 s per pass, enforced in an RPC or Edge Function — not only in the UI.'),
          mk(now, 'Pass-word lockout', 'Hash with bcrypt/argon2. 5 fails → 60 s cooldown per pass + per IP.'),
          mk(now, 'Report + block', 'reports(target, reason, reporter_pass). 3 reports auto-hide. Block revokes the pass.'),
          mk(now, 'Viewer link', 'view_links(event_id, slug, show_names, show_comments, allow_download, revoked_at).')
        ] },
        { title: '03 · Location & moderation', bg: '#161618', ink: '#F4F4F2', sub: '#A5A5AC', items: [
          mk(now, 'Venue via Google Places', 'Places Autocomplete → store place_id, name, address, lat/lng on events. Never store guests’ GPS.'),
          mk(now, 'Map preview + Open in Maps', 'Maps Static API image for the card (cheap, no JS). Link to google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query_place_id=…'),
          mk(now, 'Hide-location switch', 'events.show_location — off shows venue to host & team only.'),
          mk(next, 'Text moderation', 'Profanity mask + toxicity score on comments/captions before insert.'),
          mk(next, 'Image moderation', 'AWS Rekognition DetectModerationLabels on upload; above threshold → hidden + review queue. Check current per-image pricing.')
        ] }
      ]
    };
  }
}
</script>
